尊敬的各位股東：                               Dear Shareholders,

2023年，在充滿挑戰的宏觀經濟和資本市場環境                Despite the challenging macroeconomic and capital market
下，珍酒李渡集團於2023年4月27日在香港聯交               environment in 2023, ZJLD Group successfully listed on the
所成功上市，成為近8 年第一家成功上市的白酒                 Hong Kong Stock Exchange on April 27, 2023 and completed
企業和該年度香港聯交所融資規模最大的IPO。                 Hong Kong’s largest IPO of the year. ZJLD Group is also the first
這一里程碑式的突破離不開眾多股東對我們的信                  baijiu company to have listed in nearly eight years. On behalf
任和支持，我代表珍酒李渡集團對諸位表示最誠                  of ZJLD Group, I would like to extend our sincere gratitude
摯的感謝。珍酒李渡全體同仁將會繼續踐行「實                  to our Shareholders, as we could not have achieved such a
業興國」和長期主義的精神，持續回報我們的股                  remarkable milestone without their trust and support. We will
東、經銷商、消費者和社會。                          continue to uphold the spirit of “invigorating our nation with
                                       entrepreneurship” and “long-termism”, as well as give back to
                                       our Shareholders, distributors, consumers and the society.

作為珍酒李渡集團的掌舵人，我和集團始終以                   Being the helmsman of ZJLD Group, I make it our mission to
「為消費者釀造美酒、傳遞快樂、構建美好生活」                 “craft fine baijiu, deliver happiness, and foster a better life for
為使命。因此，我們堅定貫徹七項原則，即：                   consumers”. As such, we firmly adhere to the seven principles
「一絲不苟釀美酒、精雕細琢造產品、耕植文化                  of “wholeheartedly brewing fine baijiu; exquisitely crafting
創品牌、誠心誠意建渠道、精心策劃搞體驗、實                  products; entrenching culture in brand development; sincerely
實在在做企業、勇擔責任促振興」。這七點也是                  developing channels; carefully creating immersive experiences;
我們堅持長期主義的例證。我們相信，只有踐行                  conscientiously operating our business; and courageously
長期主義，堅持做難但正確的事，才能引領集團                  shouldering responsibilities to prosper”. These seven principles
穿越週期，實現可持續的高品質增長。                      serve as a testament to our unwavering commitment towards
                                       long-termism. We believe that our Group will only be able to
                                       ride through cycles and achieve sustainable quality growth, by
                                       upholding long-termism and staying committed to undertaking
                                       arduous yet virtuous endeavours.

白酒行業的競爭不斷在變化，我認為白酒企業能                  Competition in the baijiu industry is constantly evolving. For
夠從當前競爭中脫穎而出的四大關鍵要素是：品                  a baijiu company to stand out from the current competition,
牌、產能、渠道和團隊。集團也圍繞這四大要素                  the four elements that I consider key are: brand development,
制定了清晰的戰略，並正以極高的決心和行動力                  production capacity, distribution channels and team building.
有效地貫徹執行。                               Our Group formulated clear strategies around these four
                                       elements and has been actively and effectively implementing
                                       them with great determination.

•    圍繞品牌，我們堅持以體驗式營銷為主，                •      In terms of brand development, we primarily focus on
     通過品鑒會和回廠遊等活動，沉浸式精準                       experiential marketing and engage core target consumers
     觸達核心目標消費群，從而避免過度依賴                       through immersive experiences, such as tasting events and
     無差別廣告投放。通過幾年的積累，珍酒                       distillery tours, to avoid over-reliance on undifferentiated
     和李渡 品牌已在目標客群中建立了鮮明的                      advertising approaches. Through years of effort, Zhen Jiu
     品牌調性，品牌號召力大幅提升，這也是                       and Li Du have established a distinctive brand identity
     我們持續快速增長的基礎。                             within our target consumer groups and significantly
                                              enhanced their brand appeal, laying the foundation for
                                              our continued rapid growth.

擎。根據弗若斯特沙利文的數據，按2023年收入               baijiu being our major growth engine. According to Frost &
計，我們是中國第三大民營白酒公司。                     Sullivan, we were the third largest private-owned baijiu company
                                      in China, in terms of revenue in 2023.

們開發了中端價位的白酒產品，以迎合對價格實                 our premium and above product portfolio and increase the

優化我們的經銷商群體；(vii)通過我們優秀的白              promote our product turnover in the market; (v) we sought to
酒研發團隊，並與專業機構合作，我們在基酒釀                 maintain inventory held by our distributors at a healthy level

巨大。我們的李渡 產品以標誌性的口感及優良品                   significant growth potential. Our Li Du products distinguish
質、源於中國傳統文化的獨特沉浸式體驗方式以                    themselves from the other baijiu products by their signature

獨樹一幟。憑藉這些特點，李渡 在其基礎市場江                   stemming from the traditional Chinese culture, and premium
西省站穩陣腳，並正在戰略性地向全國擴張，以                    brand positioning and operation. With these features, Li Du has

文的數據，按2023年收入計，李渡 為中國第五大                 and is strategically expanding its geographic reach across China
兼香型白酒品牌，並在五大兼香型白酒品牌中實                    to support its incremental and long-term growth. Li Du was the

該品牌的創立乃為了致敬其起源地江西省李渡                     It was established to honor its origin from Lidu, Jiangxi Province,
鎮 － 一座有著悠久白酒釀造傳統的中國古鎮。                   an ancient town in China that is steeped in its long-standing
李渡 白酒釀造工藝被認定為江西省非物質文化遺                   tradition of making baijiu. The baijiu-making techniques of
產。於 2002 年，在我們於江西省李渡鎮翻新我                 Li Du were recognized as an Intangible Cultural Heritage of
們的生產基地時發現了一個古代元朝（公元1271                  Jiangxi. In 2002, an ancient baijiu distillery of the Yuan Dynasty
年至公元1368 年）白酒釀酒廠，該酒廠隨後被列                 (1271 AD to 1368 AD) was discovered during the renovation
入全國重點文物保護單位名單。於2023年4月27                 of our production facility in Lidu, Jiangxi Province and was
日，李渡 與其他六家領先的白酒企業共同申請將                   subsequently named on the list of Major National Historical and
中國白酒列入中國世界文化遺產暫定目錄。                      Cultural Sites. On April 27, 2023, Li Du , together with six other
                                         leading baijiu companies, jointly applied for Chinese baijiu to
                                         be included in China’s tentative list of world cultural heritage.

產品組合及成本結構，我們力爭2024年保持健康               strengthening our overall production capabilities. With our
增長和提高盈利能力。                            continuous optimization of our sales network and distribution
                                      channels, our emphasis on end-to-end marketing and our efforts
                                      to optimize our product mix and cost structure, we strive to
                                      maintain our healthy growth and improve our profitability in
                                      2024.

展望未來，在高端化趨勢持續、醬香型白酒興起                 Looking forward, the Group remains confident in the long-term
及市場整合的推動下，本集團對中國白酒行業的                 development of China’s baijiu industry and the Group’s business
長遠發展及本集團2024年的業務仍充滿信心。                in 2024, driven by the continuous trends of premiumization,
                                      rising of sauce aroma baijiu and market consolidation.

我們的銷售成本主要包括(i)生產材料成本，包括                 Our cost of sales primarily consisted of (i) cost of production
原材料成本、折舊及攤銷以及負責白酒生產的人                   materials, including cost of raw materials, depreciation and
員的員工薪酬；(ii)用於生產白酒產品的包裝物料                amortization and employee compensation for our personnel
成本；(iii) 稅項及附加費，主要是與我們的白酒               responsible for baijiu production, (ii) cost of packaging
產品銷售相關的消費稅；及(iv)運輸成本，主要指                materials used to produce our baijiu products, (iii) tax and
交付白酒產品的物流開支。                            surcharges, primarily consumption tax charged in connection
                                        with sales of our baijiu products, and (iv) transportation cost,
                                        mainly representing logistics expenses for delivery of our baijiu
                                        products.

外匯風險                                    Foreign Exchange Exposure
截至2023 年12 月31 日止年度，本集團在中國開             During the year ended December 31, 2023, the Group
展業務，而交易以人民幣結算。本集團在中國的                   conducted its business in China with the transactions settled in
附屬公司的功能貨幣為人民幣。因相關業務在其                   Renminbi. The functional currency of the Group’s subsidiaries in
各自之營運上形成自然對沖機制，外匯風險相對                   the PRC is RMB. As there is a natural hedge mechanism in place
較低，故本集團目前並無任何金融工具作對沖用                   during the course of its respective business operation and the
途。為加強整體風險管理，本集團將繼續留意外                   impact of the foreign exchange risk is low, the Group currently
匯風險，並採取審慎措施緩解外匯風險且於必要                   does not have any financial instruments for hedging purposes.
時採取適當行動。                                To enhance overall risk management, the Group will continue
                                        to keep track of the foreign exchange risk and take prudent
                                        measures to mitigate exchange risk, and take appropriate action
                                        where necessary.

首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃                           POST-IPO EQUITY INCENTIVE PLAN
首次公開發售後股權激勵計劃於2023年4月11日                The Post-IPO Equity Incentive Plan was approved by way of
經股東決議案獲批准，並自上市日期起生效。該                   shareholders’ resolution on April 11, 2023, with effect from
計劃的目的是激勵合資格參與者並就彼等對本集                   the Listing Date. The purpose of this plan is to incentivize and
團作出的貢獻給予獎勵，使彼等的利益與本公司                   reward eligible participants for their contribution to the Group
利益一致，藉此鼓勵彼等努力提升本公司價值。                   and to align their interests with that of the Company, so as to
                                        encourage them to work towards enhancing the value of the
                                        Company.

董事會欣然提呈本公司截至2023 年12 月31 日止             The Board is pleased to present the Corporate Governance
年度的年度報告所載之企業管治報告。                       Report contained in the Company’s annual report for the year
                                        ended December 31, 2023.

企業管治常規                                  CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES
本公司深知企業管治是卓越運營的基石。我們不                   The Company understands that corporate governance is
斷完善企業管治架構，高度重視內外部企業風險                   the cornerstone of excellent operations. We have been
管理及合規工作。我們亦一直致力於加強董事                    continuously refining our corporate governance structure,
會層面的ESG工作，同時密切監察持份者對我們                  placing high importance on enterprise risk management and
ESG工作的期望及要求。展望未來，我們的目標                  compliance both internally and externally. We have also been
是不斷優化我們的管治體系，提高管治水平，滿                   persistently working on enhancing ESG efforts at board level,
足監管要求，並營造一個透明、多元化和值得信                   while closely monitoring the expectations and demands of
賴的企業環境。                                 stakeholders regarding our ESG work. Looking forward, we
                                        aim to continuously optimize our governance system, enhance
                                        governance standards, meet regulatory requirements, and foster
                                        a transparent, diverse and trustworthy corporate environment.

本公司的企業管治常規以企業管治守則所載的原                   The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on the
則及守則條文為基礎，且本公司已採納企業管治                   principles and code provisions as set out in the CG Code and the
守則的守則條文作為其本身的企業管治守則。董                   Company has adopted the code provisions of the CG Code as its
事會認為，自上市日期起至2023 年12 月31 日，             own code of corporate governance. The Board is of the view that

董事會多元化政策                                  BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY
本集團已採納董事會多元化政策，該政策載列我                     The Group has adopted the board diversity policy which sets out
們實現及維持董事會多元化的方法。根據該政                      our approach to achieve and maintain diversity of the Board.
策，我們在選擇董事會候選人時會考慮多項因                      Pursuant to this policy, we seek to achieve board diversity
素，包括（惟不限於）性別、年齡、文化及教育背                    through the consideration of a number of factors when selecting
景、種族、專業經驗、技能、知識和服務年期，                     the candidates to the Board, including but not limited to gender,
以求達致董事會多元化。最終人選將基於候選人                     age, cultural and educational background, ethnicity, professional
可為董事會帶來的裨益及貢獻而決定。提名委員                     experience, skills, knowledge and length of service. The ultimate
會負責檢討董事會的多元化。                             decision of the appointment will be based on merit and the
                                          contribution which the selected candidates will bring to the
                                          Board. Our Nomination Committee is responsible for reviewing
                                          the diversity of the Board.

The Group’s sales contracts with customers have a                條款妥為確認；
variety of trade terms relating to goods acceptance.      •      comparing, on a sample basis, sales transactions
Such trade terms may affect the timing of the                    recorded during the financial reporting period
recognition of sales to those customers. Management              with the corresponding invoices, goods delivery
evaluates the trade terms of each contract to determine          notes, confirmations from distributors and other
the timing of revenue recognition.                               relevant supporting documents, which contained
                                                                 evidence of acknowledgement of the customers’
我們將收入確認時間釐定為關鍵審計事項，因為收入                                          receipt of the goods, and assessing if the related
是 貴集團的關鍵績效指標，且存在為滿足財務預期或                                         revenue was properly recognised in accordance

收入可能無法在正確的期間內妥為確認。
We identified the timing of revenue recognition as a
key audit matter because revenue is a key performance
indicator of the Group and there is an inherent risk
that revenue may be manipulated to meet financial
expectations or targets. In addition, different types
of customers with different trade terms give rise to
complexity in timing of revenue recognition, and, as a
result, revenue may not be properly recognised in the
correct periods.

 1      公司資料                                       1    CORPORATE INFORMATION
        珍 酒 李 渡 集 團 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」）於                 ZJLD Group Inc (the “Company”) was incorporated in the
        2021 年9 月24 日根據開曼群島公司法（經                        Cayman Islands as an exempted company with limited
        修訂）於開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限                              liability under the Companies Act (as revised) of the
        公司。本公司股份於2023年4月27日在香                           Cayman Islands on 24 September 2021. The Company’s
        港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上                            shares were listed on the Main Board of The Stock
        市。本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）                            Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”)
        主要從事釀造、生產及銷售白酒產品。                               on 27 April 2023. The Company and its subsidiaries
                                                        (together, the “Group”) are principally engaged in the
                                                        making, production and sales of baijiu products.

 2    重大會計政策（續）                              2    MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES
                                                  (Continued)
      (b)     財務報表的編製基準                           (b)   Basis of preparation of the financial
                                                        statements
              截至2023 年12 月31 日止年度綜合                     The consolidated financial statements for the year
              財務報表包括本集團。                                ended 31 December 2023 comprise the Group.

              編製財務報表所用的計量基準為歷                           The measurement basis used in the preparation of
              史成本基準，惟衍生金融工具乃按                           the financial statements is the historical cost basis,
              其公允價值列賬（見附註2(e)）。                         except for derivative financial instruments which
                                                        are stated at their fair values (see Note 2(e)).

              編製符合國際財務報告會計準則的                           The preparation of financial statements in
              財務報表需要本集團的管理層作出                           conformity with IFRS Accounting Standards
              判斷、估計及假設，而該等判斷、                           requires management to make judgements,
              估計及假設會影響政策的應用以及                           estimates and assumptions that affect the
              資產、負債、收入及開支的呈報金                           application of policies and reported amounts
              額。該等估計及相關假設乃根據過                           of assets, liabilities, income and expenses. The
              往經驗及在有關情況下認為合理的                           estimates and associated assumptions are based
              多項其他因素而作出，其結果構成                           on historical experience and various other factors
              對未能從其他來源確定的資產及負                           that are believed to be reasonable under the
              債的賬面值作出判斷的基準。實際                           circumstances, the results of which form the basis
              結果可能有別於該等估計。                              of making the judgements about carrying values of
                                                        assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent
                                                        from other sources. Actual results may differ from
                                                        these estimates.

              估計及相關假設會持續覆核。倘會                           The estimates and underlying assumptions are
              計估計的修訂僅對作出修訂的期間                           reviewed on an ongoing basis. Revisions to
              產生影響，則有關修訂只會在該期                           accounting estimates are recognised in the period
              間內確認；倘會計估計的修訂對                            in which the estimate is revised if the revision
              現時及未來期間均產生影響，則會                           affects only that period, or in the period of the
              在作出該修訂期間及未來期間內確                           revision and future periods if the revision affects
              認。                                        both current and future periods.

              管理層應用國際財務報告會計準則                           Judgements made by management in the
              時作出的對財務報表具有重大影響                           application of IFRS Accounting Standards that
              的判斷及估計不確定性的主要來源                           have significant effect on the financial statements
              於附註3討論。                                   and major sources of estimation uncertainty are
                                                        discussed in Note 3.

      (g)     租賃資產                                (g)   Leased assets
              本集團於合同初始評估有關合同是                           At inception of a contract, the Group assesses
              否屬租賃或包含租賃。倘合同為換                           whether the contract is, or contains, a lease. This
              取代價而給予在一段時間內控制已                           is the case if the contract conveys the right to
              識別資產使用的權利，則該合同是                           control the use of an identified asset for a period
              租賃或包含租賃。在客戶有權指示                           of time in exchange for consideration. Control is
              使用已識別資產，並有權從該用途                           conveyed where the customer has both the right to
              獲得幾乎所有經濟利益的情況下，                           direct the use of the identified asset and to obtain
              則擁有控制權。                                   substantially all of the economic benefits from that
                                                        use.

              作為承租人                                     As a lessee
              就所有租賃而言，倘合同包含租賃                           Where the contract contains lease component(s)
              組成部分及非租賃組成部分，本集                           and non-lease component(s), the Group has
              團選擇不區分非租賃組成部分及將                           elected not to separate non-lease components
              每個租賃組成部分及任何相關的非                           and accounts for each lease component and any
              租賃組成部分列為單一的租賃組成                           associated non-lease components as a single lease
              部分。                                       component for all leases.

              於租賃開始日期，本集團確認使用                           At the lease commencement date, the Group
              權資產及租賃負債，惟租期12個月                          recognises a right-of-use asset and a lease liability,
              或以下的短期租賃及低價值資產租                           except for short-term leases that have a lease term
              賃除外。當本集團就低價值資產訂                           of 12 months or less and leases of low-value assets.
              立租賃時，本集團就每份租賃決定                           When the Group enters into a lease in respect of
              是否將租賃資本化。與未資本化租                           a low-value asset, the Group decides whether to
              賃相關的租賃付款會在租賃期內有                           capitalise the lease on a lease-by-lease basis. The
              系統地確認為開支。                                 lease payments associated with those leases which
                                                        are not capitalised are recognised as an expense
                                                        on a systematic basis over the lease term.

              當將租賃資本化時，租賃負債初步                           Where the lease is capitalised, the lease liability
              以租期內應付租賃付款使用租賃隱                           is initially recognised at the present value of
              含的利率或（倘利率無法即時釐定）                          the lease payments payable over the lease term,
              相關增量借貸利率貼現的現值確                            discounted using the interest rate implicit in the
              認。於初步確認後，租賃負債按攤                           lease or, if that rate cannot be readily determined,
              銷成本計量，而利息開支使用實際                           using a relevant incremental borrowing rate. After
              利率法計算。計量租賃負債時並無                           initial recognition, the lease liability is measured at
              計入並非取決於指數或利率的可變                           amortised cost and interest expense is calculated

                    概率加權估計。一般而言，                              of credit losses. Generally, credit losses

                    差額（即合同金額與預期金                              expected cash shortfalls between the
                    額之間的差額）的現值計量。                             contractual and expected amounts.

                    倘貼現的影響屬重大，則貿                              The expected cash shortfalls of trade and
                    易及其他應收款項的預期現                              other receivables are discounted using the
                    金差額採用初始確認時釐定                              effective interest rate determined at initial
                    的實際利率或相似利率貼                               recognition or an approximation thereof
                    現。                                        where the effect of discounting is material.

                    的最長期限，乃本集團面臨                              estimating ECLs is the maximum contractual

                      信貸風險大幅上升                                      Significant increases in credit risk
                      釐定金融工具的信貸風險自                                  When determining whether the credit risk
                      初始確認以來是否大幅上升                                  of a financial instrument has increased
                      及於計量預期信貸虧損時，                                  significantly since initial recognition and
                      本集團考慮無需付出過多成                                  when measuring ECLs, the Group considers
                      本及努力下可獲得的合理及                                  reasonable and supportable information that

        (i)    存貨                                       (i)   Inventories
               存貨為於日常業務過程中持作出售                                Inventories are assets which are held for sale in

               者入賬。                                           net realisable value.

               使存貨達至現時所在地點及保持現                                of conversion and other costs incurred in bringing
               有狀況所涉及的成本。                                     the inventories to their present location and
                                                              condition.

               所需的估計成本。                                       estimated costs of completion and the estimated
                                                              costs necessary to make the sale.

               存貨被出售時，該等存貨的賬面值                                When inventories are sold, the carrying amount
               確認為相關收入確認期間的開支。                                of those inventories is recognised as an expense
                                                              in the period in which the related revenue is

               項。                                             reduction in the amount of inventories recognised

              外。                                        also disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the

               就財務報告而言，個別重要經營分                                Individually material operating segments are not
               部不會合併計算，除非有關分部具                                aggregated for financial reporting purposes unless
               有類似經濟特徵以及產品及服務性                                the segments have similar economic characteristics
               質、生產程序性質、客戶類型或類                                and are similar in respect of the nature of products
               別、分銷產品或提供服務採用的方                                and services, the nature of production processes,
               法及監管環境性質類似。倘個別不                                the type or class of customers, the methods used
               重要的經營分部符合上述大部分標                                to distribute the products or provide the services,
               準，則可予以合併計算。                                    and the nature of the regulatory environment.
                                                              Operating segments which are not individually
                                                              material may be aggregated if they share a majority
                                                              of these criteria.

               斷。該等估計的變動可能會對未來                                customer’s historical rebate rates, accumulated

 25     財務風險管理及金融工具公允                              25   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT
        價值                                              AND FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL
                                                        INSTRUMENTS
        本集團在日常業務過程中面臨信貸、流動                              Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency
        資金、利率及貨幣風險。                                     risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.

        下文呈列本集團所承受的該等風險及本集                              The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk
        團管理該等風險所採用的財務風險管理政                              management policies and practises used by the Group to
        策及慣例。                                           manage these risks are described below.

        (a)    信貸風險                                     (a)   Credit risk
               信貸風險指交易對手未履行其合同                                Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will
               責任而導致本集團產生財務虧損的                                default on its contractual obligations resulting in a
               風險。本集團的信貸風險主要來自                                financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk
               貿易應收款項。本公司董事認為，                                is primarily attributable to trade receivables. The
               由於對手方為信譽良好的銀行及本                                directors of the Company are of the opinion that
               集團認為彼等的信貸風險較低，因                                the Group’s exposure to credit risks arising from
               此本集團因銀行及手頭現金以及應                                cash at bank and on hand and bills receivables
               收票據面臨的信貸風險有限。本集                                is limited because the counterparties are banks
               團並無提供將令本集團面臨信貸風                                with good credit standing, for which the Group
               險的任何擔保。                                        considers to have low credit risk. The Group does
                                                              not provide any guarantees which would expose
                                                              the Group to credit risk.

 25   財務風險管理及金融工具公允                          25   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT
      價值（續）                                       AND FAIR VALUES OF FINANCIAL
                                                  INSTRUMENTS (Continued)
      (a)     信貸風險（續）                             (a)   Credit risk (Continued)
              貿易應收款項                                    Trade receivables
              本集團已設立信貸風險管理政策，                           The Group has established a credit risk
              據此，所有要求信貸超過其各自經                           management policy under which individual
              批准信貸限額的客戶均須進行個別                           credit evaluations are performed on all customers
              信貸評估。該等評估專注於客戶過                           requiring credit over their respective approved
              往支付到期款項的歷史及現有償還                           credit limits. These evaluations focus on the
              能力，並考慮客戶的特定資料。貿                           customer’s past history of making payments when
              易應收款項一般於開票日期起計30                          due and current ability to pay, and take into
              日內到期，其中向若干大客戶授予                           account information specific to the customer.
              一個月至一年的信用期。餘款逾期                           Trade receivables are usually due within 30 days
              3 個月以上的債務人須於獲授任何                          from the date of billing, where credit periods of
              進一步信貸額前清償所有未償還結                           one month to one year are granted to certain large
              餘。本集團通常不會從客戶處獲得                           customers. Debtors with balances that are more
              抵押品。                                      than 3 months past due are requested to settle
                                                        all outstanding balances before any further credit
                                                        is granted. Normally, the Group does not obtain
                                                        collateral from customers.

              本集團承受個人客戶的重大風險                            Significant concentrations of credit risk primarily
              時，將產生高度集中的信貸風險。                           arise when the Group has significant exposure
              於報告期末，貿易應收款項總額中                           to individual cus tomers . A t the end o f the
              5%（2022 年：5%）應收自本集團                       reporting period, 5% (2022: 5%) of the total trade
              最大債務人，及貿易應收款項總額                           receivables was due from the Group’s largest
              中19%（2022 年：12%）應收自本                      debtor, and 19% (2022: 12%) of the total trade
              集團前五大債務人。                                 receivables, were due from the Group’s five largest
                                                        debtors.

              本集團按等於整個存續期預期信貸                           The Group measures loss allowance for trade
              虧損（使用撥備矩陣計算）的金額計                          receivables at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs,
              量貿易應收款項的虧損撥備。由於                           which is calculated using a provision matrix. As the
              本集團的歷史信貸虧損情況並無顯                           Group’s historical credit loss experience indicate
              示不同客戶類型的虧損模式有重大                           no significant different loss patterns for different
              差異，因此基於逾期狀態的虧損撥                           types of customer, the loss allowance based on
              備並無於本集團不同客戶類型間區                           past due status is not distinguished among the
              分。                                        Group’s different customer types.
